[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

... (showing lines 1-118 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge

         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

Now let me create an optimized version with a narrowed accumulator (the max sum of 8 products of 8-bit signed values fits in 19 bits, not 32).


  -> create_file(filename='v1.v', content="// v1: systolic TPU MXU with narrow (19-bit) internal accumulator.\n// Sum of 8 products of signed 8x8 values lies in [-131072, 131072] => 19 bits.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input [ACCW-1:0]\t psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t a_east,\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north +\n                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\n    input\t\t en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,   // external result width\n    parameter AW   = 19)   // internal accumulator width\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(AW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [AW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*AW +: AW];\n         wire [AW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [AW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){cout[AW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
Now let me try a fundamentally different structure: broadcast A (no horizontal chain), register products, and use a pipelined adder chain. This separates the multiply and add onto different pipeline stages, shortening the critical path.


  -> create_file(content="// v2: broadcast-A TPU tile.\n// Instead of a systolic mesh, A is delayed and broadcast; all 64 products are\n// formed in parallel and summed by a per-column pipelined adder chain.\n// Latency budget 15 = 7 (A delay) + 1 (product reg) + 7 (adder chain).\n// Critical path per stage is either one 8x8 multiply or one 19-bit add.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, AW = 19, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam ND  = 7;\t\t// activation delay stages\n   localparam NC  = 7;\t\t// adder chain stages\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line (zeros injected while a_valid is low).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [N*DW-1:0] adel [0:ND-1];\n   integer\t  k;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (k = 0; k < ND; k = k + 1)\n           adel[k] <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         adel[0] <= a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n         for (k = 1; k < ND; k = k + 1)\n           adel[k] <= adel[k-1];\n      end\n   end\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_cur = adel[ND-1];\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights: vertical shift register loaded from the top.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\ti, j;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1)\n           for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1)\n             wreg[i][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (ld) begin\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1)\n           wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n         for (i = 1; i < N; i = i + 1)\n           for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1)\n             wreg[i][j] <= wreg[i-1][j];\n      end\n   end\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Product registers (one 8x8 signed multiply each, then registered).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg signed [2*DW-1:0] preg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\t\t i2, j2;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (i2 = 0; i2 < N; i2 = i2 + 1)\n           for (j2 = 0; j2 < N; j2 = j2 + 1)\n             preg[i2][j2] <= {(2*DW){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         for (i2 = 0; i2 < N; i2 = i2 + 1)\n           for (j2 = 0; j2 < N; j2 = j2 + 1)\n             preg[i2][j2] <= $signed(a_cur[i2*DW +: DW]) * $signed(wreg[i2][j2]);\n      end\n   end\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per-column pipelined adder chain: acc[0] = p0+p1, acc[k] = acc[k-1]+p(k+1).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg signed [AW-1:0] acc [0:NC-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\t       s, j3;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (s = 0; s < NC; s = s + 1)\n           for (j3 = 0; j3 < N; j3 = j3 + 1)\n             acc[s][j3] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         for (j3 = 0; j3 < N; j3 = j3 + 1) begin\n            acc[0][j3] <= $signed(preg[0][j3]) + $signed(preg[1][j3]);\n            for (s = 1; s < NC; s = s + 1)\n              acc[s][j3] <= acc[s-1][j3] + $signed(preg[s+1][j3]);\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\n   genvar jj;\n   generate\n      for (jj = 0; jj < N; jj = jj + 1) begin : g_out\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] r = acc[NC-1][jj];\n         assign c_data[jj*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){r[AW-1]}}, r};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v2.v')

The bug: my adder chain needs each product skewed by one extra cycle per stage. Let me instead use a balanced pipelined tree where all products are available simultaneously — with more cycles devoted to the cheap 8-bit A delay line.




  -> create_file(content="// v3: broadcast-A TPU tile with pipelined adder tree.\n//\n// All 64 products for one A row are formed in parallel (one 8x8 signed\n// multiply per PE site), registered, then reduced by a balanced 3-level adder\n// tree. Pipeline budget 15 = 11 (8-bit A delay line) + 1 (product reg)\n// + 3 (tree levels). Critical path per stage is a single multiply or a single\n// narrow add.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, AW = 19, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam ND  = 11;\t\t// activation delay depth\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line (zeros injected while a_valid is low).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [N*DW-1:0] adel [0:ND-1];\n   integer\t  k;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (k = 0; k < ND; k = k + 1)\n           adel[k] <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         adel[0] <= a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n         for (k = 1; k < ND; k = k + 1)\n           adel[k] <= adel[k-1];\n      end\n   end\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_cur = adel[ND-1];\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights: vertical shift register loaded from the top.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\ti, j;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1)\n           for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1)\n             wreg[i][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (ld) begin\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1)\n           wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n         for (i = 1; i < N; i = i + 1)\n           for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1)\n             wreg[i][j] <= wreg[i-1][j];\n      end\n   end\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Product registers (16-bit signed).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg signed [2*DW-1:0] preg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\t\t i2, j2;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (i2 = 0; i2 < N; i2 = i2 + 1)\n           for (j2 = 0; j2 < N; j2 = j2 + 1)\n             preg[i2][j2] <= {(2*DW){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         for (i2 = 0; i2 < N; i2 = i2 + 1)\n           for (j2 = 0; j2 < N; j2 = j2 + 1)\n             preg[i2][j2] <= $signed(a_cur[i2*DW +: DW]) * $signed(wreg[i2][j2]);\n      end\n   end\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Adder tree, level 1: 8 -> 4 sums of two 16-bit products (17 bits).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg signed [2*DW:0] s1 [0:N/2-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\t      a1, j3;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (a1 = 0; a1 < N/2; a1 = a1 + 1)\n           for (j3 = 0; j3 < N; j3 = j3 + 1)\n             s1[a1][j3] <= {2*DW+1{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         for (a1 = 0; a1 < N/2; a1 = a1 + 1)\n           for (j3 = 0; j3 < N; j3 = j3 + 1)\n             s1[a1][j3] <= preg[2*a1][j3] + preg[2*a1+1][j3];\n      end\n   end\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Level 2: 4 -> 2 sums (18 bits).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg signed [2*DW+1:0] s2 [0:N/4-1][0:N-1];\n   integer\t\t a2, j4;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (a2 = 0; a2 < N/4; a2 = a2 + 1)\n           for (j4 = 0; j4 < N; j4 = j4 + 1)\n             s2[a2][j4] <= {2*DW+2{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         for (a2 = 0; a2 < N/4; a2 = a2 + 1)\n           for (j4 = 0; Now I understand the key constraint: mid-drain weight reloads freeze in-flight partial sums, so the accumulation must be staged exactly like the systolic array. Let me build a design with broadcast weights but exact per-(i,j) A skew and 8-stage chains.




  -> create_file(content="// v5: broadcast-weight TPU tile with exact reload semantics.\n//\n// Reference behavior: P(i+1,j,t+1) = P(i,j,t) + a(t-i-j)[i]*w(i,j).  A weight\n// reload freezes every partial sum, so the 8-stage accumulation chain must be\n// preserved bit-exactly.  Here the weights are held in plain registers (no\n// south-shifting mesh) and the activation skew is built with cheap 8-bit shift\n// registers instead of 8-bit registers travelling through 64 PEs.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, AW = 19, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation skew: a_tap[i][j] = A row byte i delayed by (i+j) compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_tap [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_adly\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i + N - 1 == 0) begin : g_none\n            assign a_tap[i][0] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_sr\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i+N-2];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (kk = 0; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= sr[kk-1];\n               end\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tap\n               if (i + j == 0) begin : g_t0\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = din;\n               end\n               else begin : g_tn\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = sr[i+j-1];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights: B[i][j] loaded bottom row first, shifted south.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // 8-stage accumulation chain per column + output de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] prod [0:N-1];\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_prod\n            assign prod[i] = $signed(a_tap[i][j]) * $signed(wreg[i][j]);\n         end\n\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] acc [0:N-1];\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 acc[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               acc[0] <= prod[0];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 acc[kk] <= acc[kk-1] + prod[kk];\n            end\n         end\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-AW){acc[N-1][AW-1]}}, acc[N-1]};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (kk = 0; kk < D; kk = kk + 1)\n                    ds[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= acc[N-1];\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < D; kk = kk + 1)\n                    ds[kk] <= ds[kk-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v5.v')

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the key: I can split the multiply and add across different pipeline stages while keeping the exact 8-stage golden accumulation staging. Let me implement it.




  -> create_file(content="// v6: TPU tile, mult/add pipelined apart.\n//\n// Golden per-PE behavior:  P(i,j)(t+1) = P(i-1,j)(t) + A(t-i-j)[i]*B[i][j]\n// (A word arriving at time t is the row accepted at time t; a reload freezes\n// everything).  Instead of a systolic mesh the activation is fanned out from a\n// single 8-bit delay line and every weight is held in a plain register, but the\n// 8-deep accumulation cascade is preserved exactly.\n//\n// The multiply and the accumulate are placed in DIFFERENT cycles:  products for\n// i>=1 are registered one cycle ahead and read from delay taps shifted by one,\n// so every register-to-register path is either a single 8x8 multiply or a\n// single narrow add.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam ND  = 2*N - 3;\t// 13 delay-line registers\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      k;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line:  dd[d] holds the A word delayed by d cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] dd [0:ND];\n\n   assign dd[0] = din;\n   generate\n      for (i = 1; i <= ND; i = i + 1) begin : g_dly\n         reg [N*DW-1:0] q;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst)   q <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n            else if (en) q <= dd[i-1];\n         end\n         assign dd[i] = q;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (k = 0; k < N; k = k + 1)\n                 wreg[k][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (k = 1; k < N; k = k + 1)\n                 wreg[k][j] <= wreg[k-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per-column cascade: 8 registered products + 7 narrow adds.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a0 = dd[j];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a1 = dd[j+0];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a2 = dd[j+1];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a3 = dd[j+2];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a4 = dd[j+3];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a5 = dd[j+4];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a6 = dd[j+5];\n         wire signed [DW-1:0] a7 = dd[j+6];\n\n         reg signed [15:0] r0, r1, r2, r3, r4, r5, r6, r7;\n         reg signed [16:0] s1, s2;\n         reg signed [17:0] s3, s4, s5, s6;\n         reg signed [18:0] s7;\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               r0 <= 16'sd0; r1 <= 16'sd0; r2 <= 16'sd0; r3 <= 16'sd0;\n               r4 <= 16'sd0; r5 <= 16'sd0; r6 <= 16'sd0; r7 <= 16'sd0;\n               s1 <= 17'sd0; s2 <= 17'sd0;\n               s3 <= 18'sd0; s4 <= 18'sd0; s5 <= 18'sd0; s6 <= 18'sd0;\n               s7 <= 19'sd0;\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               r0 <= a0 * $signed(wreg[0][j]);\n               r1 <= a1 * $signed(wreg[1][j]);\n               r2 <= a2 * $signed(wreg[2][j]);\n               r3 <= a3 * $signed(wreg[3][j]);\n               r4 <= a4 * $signed(wreg[4][j]);\n               r5 <= a5 * $signed(wreg[5][j]);\n               r6 <= a6 * $signed(wreg[6][j]);\n               r7 <= a7 * $signed(wreg[7][j]);\n               s1 <= r0 + r1;\n               s2 <= s1 + r2;\n               s3 <= s2 + r3;\n               s4 <= s3 + r4;\n               s5 <= s4 + r5;\n               s6 <= s5 + r6;\n               s7 <= s6 + r7;\n            end\n         end\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-19){s7[18]}}, s7};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [18:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer\t      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= 19'sd0;\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= s7;\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n              {{(ACCW-19){ds[D-1][18]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v6.v')

v5 passes at 3.196M. The critical path is multiply+add in series. Let me try fusing them into a single carry-save adder tree (explicit partial products, one final CPA).


  -> create_file(content="// v7: exact golden staging, but each accumulate stage is a fused multiply-add.\n//\n// A signed 8x8 product is expanded into 8 masked partial products\n//   A*W = sum_{b=0..6} A[b]*(W<<b) - A[7]*(W<<7)\n// and the previous partial sum is added as a ninth addend, so the whole stage\n// becomes one 9-input carry-save reduction with a single final carry-propagate\n// adder instead of (multiplier CPA) + (accumulator CPA).\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation skew: a_tap[i][j] = A row byte i delayed by (i+j) compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_tap [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_adly\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i + N - 1 == 0) begin : g_none\n            assign a_tap[i][0] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_sr\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i+N-2];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (kk = 0; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= sr[kk-1];\n               end\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tap\n               if (i + j == 0) begin : g_t0\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = din;\n               end\n               else begin : g_tn\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = sr[i+j-1];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per-column cascade of fused multiply-adds + output de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] pp [0:N-1][0:DW-1];\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pp\n            wire signed [AW-1:0] wse = wreg[i][j];\n            for (kk = 0; kk < DW; kk = kk + 1) begin : g_b\n               if (kk == DW - 1)\n                 assign pp[i][kk] = a_tap[i][j][kk] ? -(wse <<< kk) : {AW{1'b0}};\n               else\n                 assign pp[i][kk] = a_tap[i][j][kk] ?  (wse <<< kk) : {AW{1'b0}};\n            end\n         end\n\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] acc [0:N-1];\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 acc[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               acc[0] <= pp[0][0]+pp[0][1]+pp[0][2]+pp[0][3]+\n                         pp[0][4]+pp[0][5]+pp[0][6]+pp[0][7];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 acc[kk] <= acc[kk-1] + pp[kk][0]+pp[kk][1]+pp[kk][2]+pp[kk][3]+\n                            pp[kk][4]+pp[kk][5]+pp[kk][6]+pp[kk][7];\n            end\n         end\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){acc[N-1][AW-1]}}, acc[N-1]};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer\t      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= acc[N-1];\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v7.v')

v7 passes at 3.06M. Now let me try splitting the multiply and add into different pipeline stages (requires ungated product registers to preserve reload semantics).


  -> create_file(content="// v8: multiply and accumulate in separate pipeline stages.\n//\n// Golden per-PE staging:  P(i,j)(T) = P(i-1,j)(T-1) + A(T-i-j)[i]*B[i][j].\n// Here the products live in their own registers (taps delayed by i+j-1) so the\n// multiply and the add never share a cycle.  The product registers are NOT\n// gated by the compute enable: during a weight reload the activation taps are\n// frozen while the new weights shift in, so the last product formed before\n// compute resumes is exactly (frozen A) * (new weight) -- matching the golden\n// array where a stalled PE keeps its a_west and picks up the reloaded weight.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n   localparam MD  = 2*N - 3;\t// deepest activation tap (13)\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line: dd[d] = A delayed by d compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] dd [0:MD];\n\n   assign dd[0] = din;\n   generate\n      for (i = 1; i <= MD; i = i + 1) begin : g_dly\n         reg [N*DW-1:0] q;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst)     q <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n            else if (en) q <= dd[i-1];\n         end\n         assign dd[i] = q;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per column: product registers + prefix-sum cascade + de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] acc [0:N-1];\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] pr  [0:N-1];\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  acc[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n                  pr[kk]  <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n            end\n            else begin\n               // ungated product registers (row 0 handled in the cascade)\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 pr[kk] <= $signed(dd[kk+j-1][kk*DW +: DW]) * $signed(wreg[kk][j]);\n               if (en) begin\n                  // acc[0] is itself a product register (row 0 of the cascade)\n                  acc[0] <= $signed(dd[j][0 +: DW]) * $signed(wreg[0][j]);\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                    acc[kk] <= acc[kk-1] + pr[kk];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n\n         // acc[0] must be built from the tap delayed by (j-1); for j == 0 the\n         // tap is the combinational input, so dd[0] is used above (j=0 -> dd[0]).\n         localparam D = N - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){acc[N-1][AW-1]}}, acc[N-1]};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer\t      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= acc[N-1];\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v8.v')

v7 passes at 3.06M. Now let me try a carry-save cascade: keep the accumulation in redundant (sum, carry) form so each stage is just a 3:2 compressor, with only one final carry-propagate adder per column. This should drastically cut both delay and area.




  -> create_file(content="// v9: exact golden staging with a REDUNDANT (carry-save) accumulation cascade.\n//\n// Golden per-PE staging:  P(i,j)(T) = P(i-1,j)(T-1) + A(T-i-j)[i]*B[i][j],\n// with a weight reload freezing every partial sum.  The numeric value of the\n// intermediate partial sums is internal, so they can be carried in redundant\n// (sum,carry) form: each stage becomes a single 3:2 compressor and only one\n// carry-propagate adder is needed per column at the end of the cascade.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation skew: a_tap[i][j] = A row byte i delayed by (i+j) compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_tap [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_adly\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i + N - 1 == 0) begin : g_none\n            assign a_tap[i][0] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_sr\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i+N-2];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (kk = 0; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= sr[kk-1];\n               end\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tap\n               if (i + j == 0) begin : g_t0\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = din;\n               end\n               else begin : g_tn\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = sr[i+j-1];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per-column redundant cascade + single CPA + output de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] p [0:N-1];\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_prod\n            assign p[i] = $signed(a_tap[i][j]) * $signed(wreg[i][j]);\n         end\n\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] s [0:N-1];\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] c [1:N-1];\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 s[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 c[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               s[0] <= p[0];\n               s[1] <= s[0] ^ p[1];\n               c[1] <= {s[0] & p[1], 1'b0};\n               for (kk = 2; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  s[kk] <= s[kk-1] ^ c[kk-1] ^ p[kk];\n                  c[kk] <= { (s[kk-1] & c[kk-1]) |\n                             (s[kk-1] & p[kk])    |\n                             (c[kk-1] & p[kk]), 1'b0 };\n               end\n            end\n         end\n\n         wire signed [AW-1:0] sum = s[N-1] + c[N-1];\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){sum[AW-1]}}, sum};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer\t      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= sum;\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v9.v')

[ASSISTANT]
Now let me implement the key optimization: separate the multiply and add into different pipeline stages, with careful tap timing and ungated product registers to preserve reload semantics.




  -> create_file(content="// v10: TPU tile with multiply and accumulate in SEPARATE pipeline stages.\n//\n// Golden per-PE behaviour (a reload freezes every partial sum):\n//   P(i,j) at end of cycle t+1 = sum_{k<=i} A(t-i-j)[k] * B[k][j]\n// so for an A row accepted in cycle T the completed column value must be\n// visible in cycle T+8+j, then de-skewed by (7-j) -> output in cycle T+15.\n//\n// Instead of a systolic mesh the activation is fanned out from per-row 8-bit\n// shift registers and the weights sit in plain registers, but the exact\n// staging is kept:\n//   tap(i,j)  = A row byte i delayed by (i+j-1) compute cycles\n//   pr(i,j)   = registered product tap(i,j)*B[i][j]     (1 stage)\n//   acc(i,j)  = acc(i-1,j) + pr(i,j)                    (8 stage cascade)\n//   de-skew   = (7-j) register stages\n// Total = (i+j-1) + 1 + (8-i) + (7-j) = 15 stages for every (i,j).\n//\n// The product registers are NOT gated by the compute enable: during a weight\n// reload the activation taps are frozen while the new weights shift in, so the\n// last product formed before compute resumes is exactly (frozen A)*(new B),\n// which is what the golden array does (a PE keeps its a_west across the stall).\n// The (0,0) element has tap delay -1: its product is combinational, matching\n// the original where PE(0,0) sees a_data directly and therefore picks up the\n// new A row on the first cycle after a reload.\n//\n// Every register-to-register path is a single 8x8 signed multiply or a single\n// narrow add.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay lines: sr[i][d] = A row byte i delayed by d+1 cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n   wire [DW-1:0]   sr [0:N-1][0:N+4];\n\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_dly\n         reg [DW-1:0] q [0:i+N-2];\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                 q[kk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               q[0] <= din[i*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                 q[kk] <= q[kk-1];\n            end\n         end\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_t\n            if (i + j == 0) begin : g_comb\n               assign sr[i][j] = din[i*DW +: DW];\n            end\n            else if (i + j == 1) begin : g_zero\n               assign sr[i][j] = din[i*DW +: DW];\n            end\n            else begin : g_reg\n               assign sr[i][j] = q[i+j-2];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per column: product registers, growing-width accumulate cascade, de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire signed [15:0] pc0 = $signed(din[0 +: DW]) * $signed(wreg[0][0]);\n\n         reg signed [15:0] pr [0:N-1];\n         integer\t      m;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (m = 0; m < N; m = m + 1)\n                 pr[m] <= 16'sd0;\n            end\n            else begin\n               if (!(j == 0)) begin : dummy_never\n               end\n               for (m = 1; m < N; m = m + 1)\n                 pr[m] <= $signed(sr[m][j]) * $signed(wreg[m][j]);\n               if (j != 0)\n                 pr[0] <= $signed(sr[0][j]) * $signed(wreg[0][j]);\n            end\n         end\n\n         // accumulate cascade (gated by the compute enable)\n         reg signed [15:0] a0;\n         reg signed [16:0] a1, a2;\n         reg signed [17:0] a3, a4, a5, a6;\n         reg signed [18:0] a7;\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               a0 <= 16'sd0;\n               a1 <= 17'sd0; a2 <= 17'sd0;\n               a3 <= 18'sd0; a4 <= 18'sd0; a5 <= 18'sd0; a6 <= 18'sd0;\n               a7 <= 19'sd0;\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               if (j == 0) a0 <= pc0;\n               else        a0 <= pr[0];\n               a1 <= a0           + pr[1];\n               a2 <= $signed(a1)  + pr[2];\n               a3 <= $signed(a2)  + pr[3];\n               a4 <= $signed(a3)  + pr[4];\n               a5 <= $signed(a4)  + pr[5];\n               a6 <= $signed(a5)  + pr[6];\n               a7 <= $signed(a6)  + pr[7];\n            end\n         end\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-19){a7[18]}}, a7};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [18:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if
