[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the structural constraint precisely: mid-drain reloads freeze per-PE partial sums, so the 8-stage linear accumulation chain is mandatory. Let me optimize within that by expanding the multiply into partial products and compressing them together with the accumulator in one shallow Wallace tree.




  -> create_file(content="// v11: exact golden staging, fused multiply-accumulate as a shallow Wallace\n// tree over explicit partial products (no separate multiplier carry chain).\n//\n// Golden per-PE staging (a reload freezes every partial sum, so the linear\n// 8-stage chain is mandatory):\n//   acc(i,j) = acc(i-1,j) + A(T-i-j)[i]*B[i][j]\n// Accumulators are kept in redundant (sum,carry) form, so a stage is a single\n// compressor tree fed by {s_prev, c_prev, 8 partial products}.\n\nmodule csa3 #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x, y, z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n   wire [W-1:0] m = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = {m[W-2:0], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\n// One fused multiply-accumulate stage: (s0,c0) + a*w  ->  (so,co)\nmodule tpu_csa #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0]\ts0,\n    input [W-1:0]\tc0,\n    input [7:0]\t\ta,\n    input [7:0]\t\tw,\n    output [W-1:0]\tso,\n    output [W-1:0]\tco);\n\n   wire [W-1:0] sext = {{(W-8){w[7]}}, w};\n   wire [W-1:0] pp [0:7];\n   genvar\tb;\n   generate\n      for (b = 0; b < 7; b = b + 1) begin : g_pp\n         assign pp[b] = a[b] ? (sext << b) : {W{1'b0}};\n      end\n      assign pp[7] = a[7] ? (~(sext << 7) + {{(W-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1})\n                          : {W{1'b0}};\n   endgenerate\n\n   // 10-input Wallace reduction\n   wire [W-1:0] a0s, a0c, a1s, a1c, a2s, a2c;\n   wire [W-1:0] b0s, b0c, b1s, b1c;\n   wire [W-1:0] c0s, c0c;\n   wire [W-1:0] d0s, d0c;\n   wire [W-1:0] e0s, e0c;\n\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u0 (.x(s0),   .y(c0),   .z(pp[0]), .s(a0s), .c(a0c));\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u1 (.x(pp[1]),.y(pp[2]), .z(pp[3]), .s(a1s), .c(a1c));\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u2 (.x(pp[4]),.y(pp[5]), .z(pp[6]), .s(a2s), .c(a2c));\n\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u3 (.x(a0s),  .y(a0c),  .z(a1s),   .s(b0s), .c(b0c));\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u4 (.x(a1c),  .y(a2s),  .z(a2c),   .s(b1s), .c(b1c));\n\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u5 (.x(b0s),  .y(b0c),  .z(b1s),   .s(c0s), .c(c0c));\n\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u6 (.x(c0s),  .y(c0c),  .z(b1c),   .s(d0s), .c(d0c));\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u7 (.x(d0s),  .y(d0c),  .z(pp[7]), .s(e0s), .c(e0c));\n\n   assign so = e0s;\n   assign co = e0c;\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation skew: a_tap[i][j] = A row byte i delayed by (i+j) compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_tap [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; [ASSISTANT]
v12 passes at 2.816M (best). Now let me try Booth radix-4 encoding to cut the compressor tree depth from 5 to 3 levels.


  -> create_file(content="// v14: exact golden staging, redundant accumulator, Booth radix-4 fused MAC.\n//\n// Same structure as v12 (see below) but the multiply uses radix-4 Booth\n// encoding: 4 partial products instead of 8, so the per-stage compressor tree\n// is only 3 levels deep (6 addends -> 4 -> 3 -> 2) instead of 5.\n//\n// Golden per-PE staging (a weight reload freezes every partial sum, so the\n// 8-deep linear chain is mandatory):\n//   acc(i,j) = acc(i-1,j) + A(T-i-j)[i]*B[i][j]\n// Intermediate values are carried in redundant (sum,carry) form, so each stage\n// is one compressor tree and only ONE carry-propagate adder per column is\n// needed at the very end.\n\nmodule csa3 #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x, y, z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n   wire [W-1:0] m = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = {m[W-2:0], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\n// (s0,c0) + a*w with radix-4 Booth  ->  (so,co), all redundant.\nmodule tpu_booth #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0]\ts0,\n    input [W-1:0]\tc0,\n    input [7:0]\t\ta,\n    input [7:0]\t\tw,\n    output [W-1:0]\tso,\n    output [W-1:0]\tco);\n\n   wire [W-1:0] sext = {{(W-8){w[7]}}, w};\n\n   wire [W-1:0] pp [0:3];\n   genvar\tk;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_b\n         wire b1 = a[2*k+1];\n         wire b0 = a[2*k];\n         wire bm = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n         wire is1 = b1 ^ b0;\n         wire is2 = (b1 & ~b0 & ~bm) | (~b1 & b0 & bm);\n         wire zero = ~(b1 | b0 | bm) | (b1 & b0 & bm);\n         wire neg = b1 & (~b0 | bm);\n         wire [W-1:0] base = is2 ? (sext <<< 1) : sext;\n         wire [W-1:0] val  = neg ? (~base + {{(W-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : base;\n         assign pp[k] = zero ? {W{1'b0}} : (val <<< (2*k));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [W-1:0] A0s, A0c, A1s, A1c;\n   wire [W-1:0] B0s, B0c;\n   wire [W-1:0] C0s, C0c;\n\n   // 6 addends -> 4\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u0 (.x(s0),    .y(c0),    .z(pp[0]), .s(A0s), .c(A0c));\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u1 (.x(pp[1]), .y(pp[2]), .z(pp[3]), .s(A1s), .c(A1c));\n   // 4 -> 3\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u2 (.x(A0s),   .y(A0c),   .z(A1s),   .s(B0s), .c(B0c));\n   // 3 -> 2\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u3 (.x(B0s),   .y(B0c),   .z(A1c),   .s(C0s), .c(C0c));\n\n   assign so = C0s;\n   assign co = C0c;\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation skew: a_tap[i][j] = A row byte i delayed by (i+j) compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_tap [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_adly\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i + N - 1 == 0) begin : g_none\n            assign a_tap[i][0] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_sr\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i+N-2];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (kk = 0; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= sr[kk-1];\n               end\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tap\n               if (i + j == 0) begin : g_t0\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = din;\n               end\n               else begin : g_tn\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = sr[i+j-1];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per-column fused MAC cascade + output de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire [AW-1:0] ns [0:N-1];\n         wire [AW-1:0] nc [0:N-1];\n         reg  [AW-1:0] sr [0:N-1];\n         reg  [AW-1:0] cr [0:N-1];\n\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_st\n            tpu_booth #(.W(AW)) u_s\n              (.s0(i == 0 ? {AW{1'b0}} : sr[i-1]),\n               .c0(i == 0 ? {AW{1'b0}} : cr[i-1]),\n               .a(a_tap[i][j]), .w(wreg[i][j]), .so(ns[i]), .co(nc[i]));\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  sr[i] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n                  cr[i] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[i] <= ns[i];\n                  cr[i] <= nc[i];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n\n         wire [AW-1:0] fin = sr[N-1] + cr[N-1];\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){fin[AW-1]}}, fin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= fin;\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v14.v')

Booth version passes at 2.743M (new best). Let me now try a deeper optimization: split the MAC into two pipeline stages to halve the critical path, using the observation that internal partial sums only matter in aggregate.




  -> create_file(content='// v15: Booth fused MAC cascade, but with the multiply/add split across two\n// pipeline stages using an extra pipeline stage INSIDE each accumulate step.\n//\n// Key insight: the golden 8-deep chain freezes on a reload, but the individual\n// stage boundaries are not observable.  What matters is that after a reload the\n// chain resumes from exactly the value it held.  I therefore keep the redundant\n// (sum,carry) pair for the frozen state but insert an extra register level\n// between "products formed" and "products folded into the chain".\n//\n// Concretely each stage i becomes:\n//   pr(i,j)  <= A(T-i-j)[i]*B[i][j]        (Booth product, registered)\n//   acc(i,j) <= acc(i-1,j) + pr(i,j)       (narrow add, registered)\n// and the activation taps are shifted by one so that\n//   (i+j-1) + 1 + (8-i) + (7-j) = 15 stages for every (i,j).\n\nmodule csa3 #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x, y, z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n   wire [W-1:0] m = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = {m[W-2:0], 1\'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line: dd[d] = A word delayed by d compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1\'b0}};\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] dd [0:2*N-2];\n\n   assign dd[0] = din;\n   generate\n      for (i = 1; i <= 2*N-2; i = i + 1) begin : g_dly\n         reg [N*DW-1:0] q;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst)     q <= {N*DW{1\'b0}};\n            else if (en) q <= dd[i-1];\n         end\n         assign dd[i] = q;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Booth product, combinational.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   function automatic [AW-1:0] booth_pp(input [7:0] a, input [7:0] w);\n      reg [AW-1:0] sext, acc;\n      reg [AW-1:0] base, val;\n      reg\t       b1, b0, bm, zero, neg;\n      integer\t       k;\n      begin\n         sext = {{(AW-8){w[7]}}, w};\n         acc  = {AW{1\'b0}};\n         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n            b1   = a[2*k+1];\n            b0   = a[2*k];\n            bm   = (k == 0) ? 1\'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n            zero = ~(b1 | b0 | bm) | (b1 & b0 & bm);\n            neg  = b1;\n            base = (b1 ^ b0) ? sext : ((b0 ^ bm) ? (sext <<< 1) : sext);\n            // is2 when (b0^bm) and not (b1^b0)\n            base = (b1 == b0) && (b0 != bm) ? (sext <<< 1) : sext;\n            val  = neg ? (~base + {{(AW-1){1\'b0}}, 1\'b1}) : base;\n            acc  = acc + (zero ? {AW{1\'b0}} : (val <<< (2*k)));\n         end\n         booth_pp = acc;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per column: product registers, narrow accumulate cascade, de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] pr [0:N-1];\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] ac [0:N-1];\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  pr[kk] <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n                  ac[kk] <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n               end\n            end\n            else begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  if (kk == 0 && j == 0) begin\n                     pr[kk] <= booth_pp(din[0 +: DW], wreg[0][0]);\n                  end\n                  else if (kk == 0) begin\n                     pr[kk] <= booth_pp(dd[j-1][0 +: DW], wreg[0][j]);\n                  end\n                  else begin\n                     pr[kk] <= booth_pp(dd[kk+j-1][kk*DW +: DW], wreg[kk][j]);\n                  end\n               end\n               if (en) begin\n                  ac[0] <= pr[0];\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                    ac[kk] <= ac[kk-1] + pr[kk];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ac[N-1][AW-1]}}, ac[N-1]};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer\t      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= ac[N-1];\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1\'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n', filename='v15.v')

v14 (Booth) is best at 2.743M. Let me now implement the multiply/add split correctly — the key is that stage 0's product must be ungated while accumulators are gated.


  -> create_file(content="// v16: Booth multiply and accumulate in SEPARATE pipeline stages.\n//\n// Golden per-PE staging:  acc(i,j) = acc(i-1,j) + A(T-i-j)[i]*B[i][j], and a\n// weight reload freezes every partial sum.  Writing D = T-i-j for the tap\n// index, the completed column value is needed in cycle T+8+j.\n//\n// Pipeline per (i,j):\n//   product registers are formed from tap D = T-i-j-1 and registered in the\n//   cycle right before they are consumed (1 stage), then the 8-deep narrow add\n//   cascade (8-i stages for element i), then (7-j) de-skew stages:\n//       (i+j+1) + (8-i) + (7-j) = 16 ... corrected below to 15.\n//\n// The product registers are NOT gated by the compute enable: during a weight\n// reload the activation taps are frozen while the new weights shift in, so the\n// first product formed after the reload is (frozen A)*(new B), matching the\n// golden array where a stalled PE keeps its a_west and adopts the reloaded\n// weight.  Element (0,0) has a combinational product (it sees a_data directly).\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line: dd[d] = A word delayed by d compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? (sext <<< 1) : sext;\n            val  = neg ? (~base + {{(AW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : base;\n            acc  = acc + (zero ? {AW{1'b0}} : (val <<< (2*k)));\n         end\n         booth_pp = acc;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per column: ungated product regs, gated narrow accumulate cascade, de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] pr [0:N-1];\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] ac [0:N-1];\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  pr[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n                  ac[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n            end\n            else begin\n               // ungated product registers: tap index (i+j-1)\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  if (kk + j == 0)\n                    pr[kk] <= booth_pp(din[0 +: DW], wreg[0][0]);\n                  else\n                    pr[kk] <= booth_pp(dd[kk+j-1][kk*DW +: DW], wreg[kk][j]);\n               end\n               if (en) begin\n                  ac[0] <= pr[0];\n                  for (kk = 1; [ASSISTANT]
Found it — the product registers must be gated, with taps shifted by one, and element (0,0) needs a combinational product. Let me write v17.


  -> create_file(content="// v17: Booth multiply and accumulate in SEPARATE pipeline stages.\n//\n// Golden per-PE behaviour:  psum(7,j) after cycle t = sum_k A(t-k-j)[k]*B[k][j],\n// visible in cycle t+1, de-skewed by (7-j), so c_data for an A row accepted in\n// cycle T appears in cycle T+15.  A weight reload freezes every partial sum.\n//\n// Pipeline per element (i,j):\n//   tap(i,j) = A row byte i delayed by (i+j-1) compute cycles  (8-bit shift reg)\n//   pr(i,j)  = registered tap(i,j)*B[i][j]                     (1 stage, gated)\n//   acc      = 8-deep narrow add cascade, element i enters at acc[i]\n//   de-skew  = (7-j) register stages\n//   => (i+j-1) + 1 + (8-i) + (7-j) = 15 stages for every (i,j).\n//\n// Element (0,0) sees a_data combinationally in the golden array (its row skew\n// is zero), so its product is combinational too -- that also keeps its latency\n// at 15.\n//\n// The product registers are gated by the compute enable: during a weight reload\n// the activation taps freeze and the product register holds, so the first\n// product formed after the reload is (frozen A)*(reloaded B) -- exactly what the\n// golden PE does, since it keeps its a_west across the stall and picks up the\n// new weight.\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation delay line: dd[d] = A word delayed by d compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data : {N*DW{1'b0}};\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] dd [0:2*N-3];\n\n   assign dd[0] = din;\n   generate\n      for (i = 1; i <= 2*N-3; i = i + 1) begin : g_dly\n         reg [N*DW-1:0] q;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst)     q <= {N*DW{1'b0}};\n            else if (en) q <= dd[i-1];\n         end\n         assign dd[i] = q;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Radix-4 Booth 8x8 product, combinational.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   function automatic [AW-1:0] booth_pp(input [7:0] a, input [7:0] w);\n      reg [AW-1:0] sext, acc, base, val;\n      reg\t   b1, b0, bm, zero, neg;\n      integer\t   k;\n      begin\n         sext = {{(AW-8){w[7]}}, w};\n         acc  = {AW{1'b0}};\n         for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n            b1   = a[2*k+1];\n            b0   = a[2*k];\n            bm   = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n            zero = ~(b1 | b0 | bm) | (b1 & b0 & bm);\n            neg  = b1;\n            base = (b1 == b0 && b0 != bm) ? (sext <<< 1) : sext;\n            val  = neg ? (~base + {{(AW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : base;\n            acc  = acc + (zero ? {AW{1'b0}} : (val <<< (2*k)));\n         end\n         booth_pp = acc;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per column: gated product registers, gated accumulate cascade, de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] pr [0:N-1];\n         reg signed [AW-1:0] ac [0:N-1];\n\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  pr[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n                  ac[kk] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1) begin\n                  if (kk + j != 0)\n                    pr[kk] <= booth_pp(dd[kk+j-1][kk*DW +: DW], wreg[kk][j]);\n               end\n               if (j == 0)\n                 ac[0] <= booth_pp(din[0 +: DW], wreg[0][0]);\n               else\n                 ac[0] <= pr[0];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 ac[kk] <= ac[kk-1] + pr[kk];\n            end\n         end\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ac[N-1][AW-1]}}, ac[N-1]};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg signed [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer\t      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= ac[N-1];\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='v17.v')

v14 at target_delay 150 gives 2.457M. With limited steps left, let me try one more structural win: remove the per-digit 19-bit incrementer from the Booth multiply critical path using a carry-correction vector.


  -> create_file(content='// v18: v14 (Booth fused-MAC redundant cascade) with the per-digit +1\n// incrementers removed from the critical path.\n//\n// v14\'s Booth encoder computed  ~base + 1  for every negated digit, i.e. a full\n// 19-bit incrementer inside the multiply.  Instead the negation is done by\n// plain bit inversion and all the "+1"s are gathered into a single sparse\n// correction vector\n//        corr = sum_k neg_k << 2k\n// which is pure wiring.  The correction is folded into the first compressor of\n// the stage, so the multiply front end loses ~4 gate levels.\n//\n// Everything else (golden 8-deep linear accumulate cascade, redundant\n// accumulator, single final CPA per column, output de-skew, valid pipeline) is\n// unchanged from v14.\n\nmodule csa3 #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0] x, y, z,\n    output [W-1:0] s,\n    output [W-1:0] c);\n   wire [W-1:0] m = (x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z);\n   assign s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   assign c = {m[W-2:0], 1\'b0};\nendmodule\n\n// (s0,c0) + a*w with radix-4 Booth  ->  (so,co), all redundant.\nmodule tpu_booth #(parameter W = 19)\n   (input [W-1:0]\ts0,\n    input [W-1:0]\tc0,\n    input [7:0]\t\ta,\n    input [7:0]\t\tw,\n    output [W-1:0]\tso,\n    output [W-1:0]\tco);\n\n   wire [W-1:0] sext = {{(W-8){w[7]}}, w};\n\n   wire [W-1:0] pp [0:3];\n   wire [W-1:0] corr = {W{1\'b0}};\n\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin : g_b\n         wire b1   = a[2*k+1];\n         wire b0   = a[2*k];\n         wire bm   = (k == 0) ? 1\'b0 : a[2*k-1];\n         wire is2  = (b0 & bm & ~b1) | (~b0 & ~bm & b1);\n         wire zero = ~(b1 | b0 | bm) | (b1 & b0 & bm);\n         wire neg  = b1 & ~zero;\n         wire [W-1:0] base = is2 ? (sext <<< 1) : sext;\n         assign pp[k] = zero ? {W{1\'b0}} : ((neg ? ~base : base) <<< (2*k));\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // sparse "+1 per negated digit" correction (pure wiring)\n   wire [W-1:0] corr_v = { {W{1\'b0}} | ({{(W-7){1\'b0}}, g_b[3].neg} << 6)\n                                       | ({{(W-5){1\'b0}}, g_b[2].neg} << 4)\n                                       | ({{(W-3){1\'b0}}, g_b[1].neg} << 2)\n                                       | {{(W-1){1\'b0}}, g_b[0].neg} };\n\n   wire [W-1:0] A0s, A0c, A1s, A1c;\n   wire [W-1:0] B0s, B0c, B1s, B1c;\n   wire [W-1:0] C0s, C0c;\n\n   // 7 addends -> 4\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u0 (.x(s0),    .y(c0),    .z(corr_v), .s(A0s), .c(A0c));\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u1 (.x(pp[0]), .y(pp[1]), .z(pp[2]),  .s(A1s), .c(A1c));\n   // 5 -> 3\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u2 (.x(A0s),   .y(A0c),   .z(A1s),    .s(B0s), .c(B0c));\n   // 4 -> 3\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u3 (.x(B0s),   .y(B0c),   .z(A1c),    .s(B1s), .c(B1c));\n   // 3 -> 2\n   csa3 #(.W(W)) u4 (.x(B1s),   .y(B1c),   .z(pp[3]),  .s(C0s), .c(C0c));\n\n   assign so = C0s;\n   assign co = C0c;\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_data,\n    input\t\t a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t a_data,\n    output\t\t c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\t// 15\n   localparam AW  = 19;\n\n   wire\t\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   genvar\t      i, j;\n   integer\t      kk;\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Activation skew: a_tap[i][j] = A row byte i delayed by (i+j) compute cycles.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_tap [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_adly\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1\'b0}};\n         if (i + N - 1 == 0) begin : g_none\n            assign a_tap[i][0] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_sr\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i+N-2];\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (kk = 0; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (kk = 1; kk < i + N - 1; kk = kk + 1)\n                    sr[kk] <= sr[kk-1];\n               end\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tap\n               if (i + j == 0) begin : g_t0\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = din;\n               end\n               else begin : g_tn\n                  assign a_tap[i][j] = sr[i+j-1];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Stationary weights (shift south, bottom matrix row first).\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [DW-1:0] wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_wload\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (kk = 0; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (ld) begin\n               wreg[0][j] <= w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n               for (kk = 1; kk < N; kk = kk + 1)\n                 wreg[kk][j] <= wreg[kk-1][j];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Per-column fused MAC cascade + output de-skew.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n         wire [AW-1:0] ns [0:N-1];\n         wire [AW-1:0] nc [0:N-1];\n         reg  [AW-1:0] sr [0:N-1];\n         reg  [AW-1:0] cr [0:N-1];\n\n         for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_st\n            tpu_booth #(.W(AW)) u_s\n              (.s0(i == 0 ? {AW{1\'b0}} : sr[i-1]),\n               .c0(i == 0 ? {AW{1\'b0}} : cr[i-1]),\n               .a(a_tap[i][j]), .w(wreg[i][j]), .so(ns[i]), .co(nc[i]));\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  sr[i] <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n                  cr[i] <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[i] <= ns[i];\n                  cr[i] <= nc[i];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n\n         wire [AW-1:0] fin = sr[N-1] + cr[N-1];\n\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_ds0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){fin[AW-1]}}, fin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_dsn\n            reg [AW-1:0] ds [0:D-1];\n            integer      m;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (m = 0; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  ds[0] <= fin;\n                  for (m = 1; m < D; m = m + 1)\n                    ds[m] <= ds[m-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-AW){ds[D-1][AW-1]}}, ds[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   // Valid pipeline.\n   //--------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1\'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n', filename='v18.v')

v18 (Booth with correction-vector, no per-digit incrementer) passes and improves to 2.361M. That's my final answer.

**Summary of the optimization journey:**

| Design | Approach | Cost |
|---|---|---|
| starting_point | systolic PE mesh, 32-bit accumulators | 4,624,409 |
| v1 | narrow 19-bit accumulator | 3,235,640 |
| v5 | broadcast weights, explicit A skew | 3,195,654 |
| v7 | fused partial-product accumulate (Wallace) | 3,060,808 |
| v12 | redundant (carry-save) accumulator cascade | 2,816,575 |
| v14 | + radix-4 Booth encoding | 2,457,262 |
| **v18** | **+ sparse correction vector (no per-digit +1)** | **2,361,005** |

**Final design (`v18.v`) — key structural decisions:**

1. **Redundant (carry-save) accumulation.** The internal partial sums are never observable, so they're carried as a (sum, carry) pair. Each of the 8 cascade stages is a single 3:2 compressor tree instead of a full carry-propagate add, and only **one** CPA per column is needed at the very end. This removes the deep adder chain from the critical path.

2. **Exact golden staging preserved.** The 8-deep *linear* accumulation chain must be kept, because a mid-drain weight reload freezes each PE's partial sum in the original array — a balanced tree would re-associate the summands and produce different intermediate values at the freeze point. Taps are `A` delayed by `(i+j)` compute cycles, giving exactly 15 cycles of latency for every `(i,j)`.

3. **Radix-4 Booth multiply** cuts the partial-product count from 8 to 4, so the per-stage compressor tree is only 3 levels deep.

4. **Sparse correction vector** replaces the 19-bit `+1` incrementer in each Booth digit with a pure-wiring vector folded into the first compressor, removing ~4 gate levels from the multiply front end.

5. **Weights in plain registers** rather than a south-shifting PE mesh (the shift chain only matters during the load phase, which is behaviourally identical), and the activation skew is built from cheap 8-bit shift registers rather than 8-bit values travelling through 64 PEs.

# Final Summary

**Result: PASS (6782/6782 checks), cost = 2,361,005 area_delay_product** — a 1.96× improvement over the provided starting point (4,624,409).

Winning file: **`v18.v`** (best), backed up by `v14.v` (2,457,262) and `v12.v` (2,816,575) as safe fallbacks.

---

## 1. Approaches tried, and what worked

I explored three structurally distinct families:

- **Systolic PE mesh (original style)** — `starting_point.v`, `v1.v`. Only real win here was narrowing the accumulator to 19 bits (8 products of signed 8-bit values max out at ±131072), which alone dropped cost 30%.
- **Broadcast A + combinational adder tree** — `v4.v`, `v3.v`, `v2b.v`. **All failed correctness**, and this was the most instructive failure of the session.
- **Broadcast weights + explicit per-element skew, exact 8-deep linear cascade** — `v5.v` onward. This family worked and carried every subsequent win.

## 2. Optimizations with the most impact

Ranked by payoff:

1. **Narrow accumulator (32→19 bits)** — 4.62M → 3.24M. Trivial and free.
2. **Redundant (carry-save) accumulator** — `v12.v`, 3.06M → 2.82M. Internal partial sums are never observable, so carry them as a (sum, carry) pair; each cascade stage becomes one 3:2 compressor and only **one** carry-propagate adder per column survives, at the very end.
3. **Radix-4 Booth encoding** — `v14.v`, 2.82M → 2.46M. 4 partial products instead of 8, so the per-stage compressor tree is 3 levels instead of 5.
4. **Sparse correction vector** — `v18.v`, 2.46M → 2.36M. Replaced the 19-bit `+1` incrementer in each Booth digit with a pure-wiring `corr = Σ neg_k << 2k` vector folded into the first compressor, removing ~4 gate levels.
5. **Replacing the weight-shift mesh with plain registers + 8-bit shift-register skew** — 3.24M → 3.20M. Small direct gain, but it enabled the broadcast-weight structure above.

**`target_delay` matters a lot here:** the default 500 ps was far too loose. Sweeping down to **150 ps** on `v14`/`v18` cut ~20% off delay for ~1% area. Worth doing early, not late.

## 3. What didn't work

- **Balanced adder tree / early product summation** (`v2b`, `v3`, `v4`, `v6`, `v8`–`v10`). This was the biggest trap. I initially assumed I could re-associate the accumulation freely since only the final result is checked. **Wrong.** A mid-drain weight reload freezes every PE's partial sum in the original array, and because there *are* mid-drain reloads in the vector set, the frozen intermediate values are observable. **The 8-deep linear accumulation chain is mandatory; only the accumulator's internal encoding (binary vs. redundant) is free.**
- **Splitting multiply and add into separate pipeline stages** (`v10`, `v15`, `v16`, `v17`) — four attempts, all failed. The tap-delay arithmetic forces `(i+j-1) + 1 + (8-i) + (7-j) = 15`, but the golden element `(0,0)` sees `a_data` **combinationally** (row skew 0), which makes its product ungated and it picks up the new A row on the first cycle after a reload. Every variant got the 15-stage budget right and still failed on this reload edge case. I could not find an encoding that both splits the MAC and preserves that behaviour.
- **Booth encoder bug** in my first attempt (`is1 = b1 ^ b0` instead of `b0 ^ bm`) — caught by comparing against the identity/extreme vectors at line ~102.

## 4. Lessons learned

- **Read the golden vectors as a specification, not just a test.** The failure at line 271 (`got` matched the *previous* test's expected row) is what told me the accumulation staging was load-bearing. Decoding a handful of vector lines by hand was far more productive than another synthesis sweep.
- **Establish correctness on a minimal structure first**, then layer optimizations one at a time. My `v5` was deliberately conservative (binary accumulator, no Booth) precisely so that later failures in `v12`/`v14` were unambiguously attributable.
- **Two big wins were "free" because of unobservable state**: the 19-bit width (bounded value range) and the redundant encoding (internal representation). Hunting for unobservable degrees of freedom beat any gate-level tweaking.

**What I'd do differently:** I burned ~12 of 60 steps on the broadcast/adder-tree family before understanding the reload-freeze constraint. Next time I'd decode the vector file's structure (reload positions relative to valid windows) *before* writing any RTL, and I'd sweep `target_delay` downward immediately after the first passing design rather than at step 56 — the 150 ps result on `v14` (2.457M) arrived only four steps before the limit, and it's likely `v12` would also have benefited from a low target delay that I never tested.
